| 173 | (added 2026-10-14, round 10b) Heritage Plantation lies between the Waccamaw River and the beaches of Pawleys Island, less than three miles from the ocean; four tennis courts and four pickleball courts; the clubhouse has a fitness room. Its marina is on the Intracoastal Waterway and the Waccamaw River, with a dock master at the dock house, 40 slips and dry storage; visiting boats can stay up to 30 days; slips are rented, mostly on long-term contracts, and the marina is open to non-residents. | 40 slips; up to 30 days | Heritage Plantation Owners Association site (row 110); Heritage Plantation Marina listing on marinas.com (the marina's own text) | https://www.heritageplantation.com/ ; https://marinas.com/view/marina/4407 | Association (row 110): "a gated Lowcountry community nestled between the Waccamaw River and the beautiful beaches of Pawleys Island"; "less than three miles from the ocean"; "four tennis courts, four pickleball courts, a pool and an owners' clubhouse with a fitness room". marinas.com: Slips 40; Dry Storage; Dockmaster; Dock House; "majority of our dock space is utilized for long term contracts (open to residents & non-residents of the Heritage Plantation community)"; transients up to 30 days; "the ICW and Waccamaw River". | 2026-10-14 | 2027-10 | verified with corrected wording | 2026-10-14, round 10b independent verifier (rows 172 to 175). VERIFIED WITH CORRECTED WORDING. Association site: heritageplantation.com returned 403 to the earlier verifier because of its user agent; with a browser user agent it opens, and so do its pages /2 (Amenities) and /3 (Gracious Lifestyle). Home page, exact: 'a gated Lowcountry community nestled between the Waccamaw River and the beautiful beaches of Pawleys Island, SC'; 'four tennis courts, four pickleball courts, a pool and an owners' clubhouse with a fitness room. Located less than three miles from the ocean'. Amenities page (primary, the association's own): 'Heritage Plantation has a four-dock marina situated on the Waccamaw River'; 'The Heritage Plantation Marina is located on the Waccamaw River, part of the Atlantic Intracoastal Waterway'; 'Dock House ... contains the office for the full-time dock master'; 'The four floating docks house approximately 40 slips. Boats can be moored for reasonable daily, monthly or annual rates. Transient boats are also welcome.'; 'Boat Ramp and Dry Storage - A fenced storage area can hold at least 40 trailers'; four Har-Tru clay tennis courts; page /3 calls it 'a private marina'. marinas.com/view/marina/4407 (a directory page carrying the marina's own text, last updated Jun 08, 2026): Slips 40; Dry Storage 40; Dockmaster; Dock House; 'The majority of our dock space is utilized for long term contracts (open to residents & non-residents of the Heritage Plantation community), as available'; 'Our marina welcomes transient boaters for periods up to 30 days'. HOLDS: dock master at the dock house, about 40 slips, dry storage, four tennis courts, four pickleball courts, a fitness room, 'between the Waccamaw River and the beaches of Pawleys Island'. MEASURED: 'less than three miles from the ocean' is the association's phrase. Straight line to the OSM coastline: the map dot 2.62 mi, the east road end 2.39 mi, the west road end (river side, near the Heritage Club bounds) 3.12 mi. So it is under three miles for most of the community and 3.1 at the river end. Say 'about three miles'. SINGLE SECONDARY SOURCE, not usable as written: (1) 'visiting boats can stay up to 30 days': the 30 days is on marinas.com only; the association says only 'Transient boats are also welcome' at daily, monthly or annual rates. (2) 'open to non-residents': marinas.com only, and the association calls it 'a private marina'. The marina's own site (heritageplantationmarina.org) reset the connection. CHANGES: 'on the Intracoastal Waterway and the Waccamaw River' to 'on the Waccamaw River, part of the Intracoastal Waterway' (the river is the waterway there); '40 boat slips' to 'about 40 boat slips' (association says approximately 40); 'visiting boats can stay for up to 30 days' to 'visiting boats are welcome'; 'Slips are rented, mostly on long-term contracts, and the marina is open to people who do not live in Heritage Plantation' to 'Boats can be moored by the day, the month or the year' (cut the non-resident clause until the marina confirms it in writing); 'Boats also go into dry storage' is fine, and 'a fenced lot holds at least 40 trailers' is the association's own wording. NOTE for the writer (row 167): the association's Amenities page is a primary source for items row 167's verifier struck as secondary: four floating docks, a party deck over the water, a full-time dock master, four Har-Tru clay courts, a clubhouse fitness facility, kitchen and meeting rooms, and 'full-time onsite property manager' (text cut at 'proper'). They may return, citing the association, but the page does not need them. Writer note: The 30 days and the long-term contracts are as row 167's verifier read marinas.com; the association's own site refused that verifier (403). |

Half the lots are beyond a half mile. Say 'a quarter to three-quarters of a mile from the ocean' (the heading 'a half mile' holds for the median). (3) Missing 'straight line': 'less than half a mile from the middle of the neighborhood' (Walmart 0.28 mi, Lowe's 0.41, Gator Hole 0.39 straight) is 0.95 mi by road for the Walmart; 'Main Street's nearest block is 0.45 mile' is 1.3 mi by road; Tidewater's Cherry Grove boat ramp 'less than a mile from the middle of the neighborhood' is 0.67 mi straight but 5.2 mi, 14 min by road. (5) Myrtle Trace '0.8 mile by road': holds from the HOA address, 0.93 from the map dot; row 158's verifier wrote 'less than a mile by road'. (6) The Reserve 'a drive of under 3 miles' to the beach at Litchfield by the Sea holds (2.76 mi to the dry-sand point; the route runs Willbrook Boulevard, Retreat Beach Circle, Tide Lane, past OSM lift_gate nodes 13183604983 and 13183604984); it says nothing about who may use that beach, and no source does (rows 106, 163, 164). Willbrook 'about 3.5 miles' is 3.43; 'about 3.4' is exact. PAGE SENTENCES TO CHANGE: see the report; each is quoted in the round 10b verifier report. ## Listing evidence for rows 17 to 39 (added 2026-10-13)

Listings, not a primary source. Read 2026-10-13. For each community: the homes for sale on that date that the RE/MAX Southern Shores neighborhood search returned (up to 8 per neighborhood name, in its order; up to 3 per Grande Dunes section), each opened on both sites. Fee columns: RE/MAX Southern Shores "HOA Fees (Calculated Monthly)" and Beach Pro Team "Hoa Fees:". "not shown" means the field is absent. A Beach Pro Team page that did not return the listing (MLS 2411141, 2516838) shows empty Beach Pro Team columns. In the price rows, a legal-description pattern with alternatives is written with "or" between them.

**Verifier check of the evidence tables (2026-10-13).** All 138 listings below were read again on RE/MAX Southern Shores (`/property/<MLS#>/` pages). The Beach Pro Team pages named in the second column were also opened again, one every 10 seconds. The RE/MAX gate, fee and golf fields match this ledger for every listing. The Seasons listings used for row 14 (MLS 2603264, 2616545, 2525332) were re-opened on both sites. Six listings have no amenities field at all (MLS 2610312, 2604732, 2624323, 2625046, 2602615, 2601290), so "not marked" for them means "no data".

## Waiting for MLS

- Sale prices for the Georgetown County communities from closed MLS sales (the county table keeps only each home's last sale and no seller name, rows 52 to 56).
- Typical prices for Willbrook Plantation, Litchfield Plantation, Wachesaw Plantation, Litchfield by the Sea and Oak Pointe (see Not verified).
- Days on market and price per square foot in gated communities against the area.
- HOA fees from closed sales, not only homes for sale on 2026-10-13. The fee rows are what sellers entered on current listings.

## Search check

Searches read 2026-10-13: "gated communities in Myrtle Beach SC" and "55+ gated communities Myrtle Beach" (finders only, never sources).

| # | Result | What it answers | What it does not answer |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | homeguidemyrtlebeach.com, "Gated Communities in Myrtle Beach" | A list of names with links to listings | Fees, what homes sell for, what kind of gate, which part is gated |
| 2 | mbcoastalhomes.realgeeks.com/gated-communities/ | A listing search page | Anything from a record |
| 3 | countryclubmag.com, "Top 20 Most Expensive Gated Communities" | A ranked list of names | Sources, fees, gate kind |
| 4 | resident.com, "Why Gated Communities in Myrtle Beach Are Perfect for Retirees" | General claims, including security and insurance claims we may not make | Anything verifiable |
| 5 | Agent pages on 55+ communities | Names Carillon at Tuscany, Beachwood at the Heritage and Del Webb sections as gated | No source; row 38 found no gate on 19 Del Webb at Grande Dunes listings |

What a buyer asks that none of them answers: which communities are gated by a record or the builder, and which only in part (Barefoot, Grande Dunes, Myrtle Trace); what a home in each sold for in the last 12 months by the county's deed records; the HOA fee per community; minutes to a public beach access; who maintains the roads behind a gate (rows 1 to 4); how guests, contractors and deliveries get in (rows 5 to 7).

## Not verified

- **Gate kind** for Seaside Plantation, Covington Lake East, The Reserve and Indigo Bay: no source says whether there is a guard, a call box or an arm only. Grande Dunes: only the Cascadia section is tied by its builder to the staffed gate (row 11); the Grande Dunes Master Association's own site (a TownSq portal, grandedunescommunities.sites.townsq.io) was found but not read, and no association document saying which sections are behind which gate was found.
- **Guard hours** from listings were not recorded (seasons row 94 rule). Prestwick and Tidewater listings call their gates guarded; when the guard is there is not shown. Litchfield by the Sea is the only community with published hours (row 6).
- **Deliveries.** Only Litchfield by the Sea publishes a rule for commercial vehicles (row 7). No source read says how parcel carriers get through any other gate. Bellwood Landing listings mention a separate delivery and construction entrance (row 18).
- **Private roads in the cities.** Rows 1 to 3 are Horry County rules for developments under the county's land development regulations. Barefoot Resort, Tidewater Plantation, Seaside Plantation and Grande Dunes are inside a city by their listings' "Lot Location" ("In City Limits"); the City of North Myrtle Beach and City of Myrtle Beach rules were not read. Georgetown County's rule was not read (its Municode client lookup returned nothing). Whether each community's streets are in fact private was not checked road by road; the page can say "gated streets are usually private roads the HOA maintains" only with that limit.
- **Emergency access through gates** (fire code rules on gates across fire apparatus roads) was not researched.
- **55+ status.** Only Myrtle Trace and Seasons at Prince Creek West are known 55+ (batch 2026-10-a). No age rule was read for any other community, so the data file leaves 55+ empty for them.
- **Prices not given:**
  - Willbrook Plantation: Georgetown County's open data server reset every connection late on 2026-10-13 (tried four times).
  - Litchfield Plantation: county legal descriptions name only 22 houses (50 parcels), clearly not all of it; 2 sales.
  - Wachesaw Plantation: most of its parcels do not carry the name in the county table (the 51 that do are its Wachesaw Farms section; 3 sales).
  - Litchfield by the Sea: many sections under other names; not attempted.
  - Oak Pointe (Barefoot): no county legal description names it.
  - Cascadia (Grande Dunes): no home resale in the window (row 12).
- **Beach minutes not given:** Litchfield by the Sea (oceanfront with its own beach club; the main gate point at US 17 and Willbrook Boulevard could not be geocoded: Nominatim returned nothing). Seaside Plantation's figure (row 59) is to a city access 0.6 miles away; it is not a statement that Seaside has its own beach access.
- **Leads not checked:** Carillon at Tuscany (55+), Beachwood at the Heritage, Hunt Club at Hunters Ridge, Waterway Hills (no MLS neighborhood of that name found on the listing site), Kingston Plantation and Myrtle Beach Resort (oceanfront condo resorts; a rental company's page, visitmbr.com, gives Myrtle Beach Resort gate fees, which is not a primary source), the Waterside Pointe section of Del Webb at Grande Dunes (that ledger's note 5: a recorded rule set and a declaration clause allowing "private gate access" for Parcel C).
- **Georgetown County sale table.** Its latest SaleDate is 2026-12-29, a date in the future, so some dates in it are wrong; sales dated after 2026-09-30 were not used. The verifier should re-run one parcel against the county's deed records (georgetowndeeds.com) before the page uses rows 52 to 56.
- **Gate documents in the deed index.** A comment search of AcclaimWeb for gate words found only Seaside Plantation (rows 15) and Bellwood Landing (row 16). A search on "GATE" alone returns nothing (the index search fails on that word), so others may exist.

## Questions only the brokerage can answer

1. For each gated community you show often: is the gate staffed, a call box or a code, and at what hours? Do agents get in with a business card, a code from the listing agent, or a call to the seller?
2. How do packages and food deliveries get in at Prestwick, Tidewater, Wachesaw and the Carolina Forest gated neighborhoods (Covington Lake East, Waterway Palms, The Bluffs)?
3. Which sections of Grande Dunes are behind the staffed gate at the bridge, and which have only their own gate? Is Del Webb's Waterside Pointe gated?
4. Do buyers who ask for "gated" usually mean a staffed guardhouse, or any gate?
5. From your own closings: typical HOA fees and sale prices in Litchfield Plantation, Willbrook Plantation and Wachesaw Plantation, where the county records were incomplete.
